SEO & Structured Data\n- Search for `sitemap` files or generation: sitemap.xml, sitemap.ts, generateSitemap\n- Search for `robots.txt` or robots.ts\n- Search for `metadata`, `generateMetadata`, `Metadata` in app/ pages (Next.js metadata API)\n- Search for JSON-LD / structured data: `application/ld+json`, `schema.org`, `Product`, `BreadcrumbList`\n- Search for Open Graph tags: `openGraph`, `og:`, `twitter:card`\n- Search for canonical URLs: `canonical`, `alternates`\n- Check if there's a manifest.json or site.webmanifest\n- List ALL pages that have metadata vs those that don't\n- Check for `<head>` customization or meta tag components\n\n## 2. i18n Completeness\n- Find ALL translation message files: search for `messages/`, `.json` files with locale names (en.json, es.json, de.json)\n- Read the structure of each locale file — list TOP-LEVEL keys\n- Compare keys between en, es, de — identify MISSING keys in es and de\n- Search for `useTranslations` usage patterns — what namespaces are used?\n- Check if there's a missing translation fallback mechanism\n- Search for hardcoded English strings in .tsx components (text not using t())\n- Check date/number formatting: `Intl`, `formatDate`, `formatNumber`, locale-aware formatting\n- Check currency formatting across locales\n\n## 3. variable: '--font-inter' })\n     9→\n    10→const DEFAULT_FONTS_URL = 'https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500;600&display=swap'\n    11→\n    12→const locales = ['en', 'es', 'de'] as const\n    13→\n    14→export function generateStaticParams() {\n    15→  return locales.map((locale) => ({ locale }))\n    16→}\n    17→\n    18→export async function generateMetadata({\n    19→  params,\n    20→}: {\n    21→  params: Promise<{ locale: string }>\n    22→}): Promise<Metadata> {\n    23→  const { locale } = await params\n    24→  const localeKey = locale as 'en' | 'es' | 'de'\n    25→\n    26→  // Fetch brand config from database\n    27→  const brandConfig = await getBrandConfig()\n    28→\n    29→  const title = brandConfig.seoTitles[localeKey] || brandConfig.seoTitles.en\n    30→  const description = brandConfig.seoDescriptions[localeKey] || brandConfig.seoDescriptions.en\n    31→\n    32→  const baseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL || 'https://skapara.com'\n    33→\n    34→  return {\n    35→    title,\n    36→    description,\n    37→    alternates: {\n    38→      languages: {\n    39→        en: `${baseUrl}/en`,\n    40→        es: `${baseUrl}/es`,\n    41→        de: `${baseUrl}/de`,\n    42→        'x-default': `${baseUrl}/en`,\n    43→      },\n    44→    },\n    45→    openGraph: {\n 'de_DE' : 'en_US',\n    51→      type: 'website',\n    52→      images: [{ url: '/brand/og-image.png', width: 1200, height: 630 }],\n    53→    },\n    54→    twitter: {\n    55→      card: 'summary_large_image',\n    56→      images: ['/brand/og-image.png'],\n    57→    },\n    58→  }\n    59→}\n    60→\n    61→export default async function LocaleLayout({\n    62→  children,\n    63→  params,\n    64→}: {\n    65→  children: React.ReactNode\n    66→  params: Promise<{ locale: string }>\n    67→}) {\n    68→  const { locale } = await params\n    69→\n    70→  // Fetch theme server-side for zero FOUC\n    71→  const theme = await getActiveTheme()\n    72→  const themeCSS = theme ?     1→import { Metadata } from 'next'\n     2→import { getTranslations } from 'next-intl/server'\n     3→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin'\n     4→import { BRAND, BASE_URL } from '@/lib/store-config'\n     5→import Link from 'next/link'\n     6→import { ShopPageClient } from '@/components/shop/ShopPageClient'\n     7→import { ShopCategoryLanding } from '@/components/shop/ShopCategoryLanding'\n     8→import { getCachedCategoryCounts, setCachedCategoryCounts, getCachedCategoryTree, setCachedCategoryTree } from '@/lib/cached-queries'\n     9→import { sanitizeForLike } from '@/lib/query-sanitizer'\n    10→import {\n    11→  Breadcrumb,\n    12→  BreadcrumbList,\n    13→  BreadcrumbItem,\n    14→  BreadcrumbLink,\n    15→  BreadcrumbSeparator,\n    16→  BreadcrumbPage,\n    17→} from '@/components/ui/breadcrumb'\n    18→\n    19→interface Product {\n    20→  id: string\n    21→  title: string\n    22→  description: string\n    23→  base_price_cents: number\n    24→  currency: string\n    25→  avg_rating: number\n    26→  review_count: number\n    27→  category_id: string\n    28→  categories: { slug: string } | { slug: string }[] | null\n    29→  status: string\n    30→  created_at: string\n    31→  images: Array<{ src: string; alt: string }>\n    32→}\n    33→\n    34→/** Batch-fetch variants from product_variants table, grouped by product_id */\n    35→async function fetchVariantsByProductId(productIds: string[]) {\n    36→  if (productIds.length === 0) return new Map<string, { sizes: string[]; colors: string[]; colorImages: Record<string, string>; hasVariantPricing?: boolean; maxPrice?: number }>()\n    37→\n    38→  const { data: allVariants } = await supabaseAdmin\n    39→    .from('product_variants')\n    40→    .select('product_id, size, color, image_url, price_cents')\n    41→    .in('product_id', productIds)\n    42→    .eq('is_enabled', true)\n    43→    .eq('is_available', true)\n    44→\n    45→  const grouped = new Map<string, { sizes: Set<string>; colors: Set<string>; colorImages: Map<string, string>; prices: Set<number> }>()\n    46→  for (const v of allVariants || []) {\n    47→    if (!grouped.has(v.product_id)) {\n    48→      grouped.set(v.product_id, { sizes: new Set(), colors: new Set(), colorImages: new Map(), prices: new Set() })\n    49→    }\n    50→    const entry = grouped.get(v.product_id)!\n    51→    if (v.size) entry.sizes.add(v.size)\n    52→    if (v.color) {\n    53→      entry.colors.add(v.color)\n    54→      if (v.image_url && !entry.colorImages.has(v.color)) {\n    55→        entry.colorImages.set(v.color, v.image_url)\n    56→      }\n    57→    }\n    58→    if (v.price_cents != null) entry.prices.add(v.price_cents)\n    59→  }\n    60→\n    61→  const result = new Map<string, { sizes: string[]; colors: string[]; colorImages: Record<string, string>; hasVariantPricing?: boolean; maxPrice?: number }>()\n    62→  for (const [id, { sizes, colors, colorImages, prices }] of grouped) {\n    63→    const priceArr = [...prices]\n    64→    const hasVariantPricing = priceArr.length > 1 && Math.min(...priceArr) !== Math.max(...priceArr)\n    65→    result.set(id, {\n    66→      sizes: [...sizes],\n    67→      colors: [...colors],\n    68→      colorImages: Object.fromEntries(colorImages),\n    69→      ...(hasVariantPricing ? { hasVariantPricing: true, maxPrice: Math.max(...priceArr) / 100 } : {}),\n    70→    })\n    71→  }\n    72→  return result\n    73→}\n    74→\n    75→/** Fetch hierarchical category tree with preview images (cached) */\n    76→async function getCategoryTree(locale: string) {\n    77→  const validLocales = ['en', 'es', 'de']\n    78→  const normalizedLocale = validLocales.includes(locale) ? locale : 'en'\n    79→  const nameField = `name_${normalizedLocale}` as 'name_en' | 'name_es' | 'name_de'\n    80→\n    81→  // Check cache\n    82→  const cached = await getCachedCategoryTree()\n    83→  if (cached) {\n    84→    return (cached as any[]).map((cat: any) => ({\n    85→      ...cat,\n    86→      name: cat[nameField] || cat.name_en,\n    87→    }))\n    88→  }\n    89→\n    90→  // Fetch all active categories\n    91→  const { data: categories } = await supabaseAdmin\n    92→    .from('categories')\n    93→    .select('id, slug, parent_id, name_en, name_es, name_de, icon, image_url, sort_order')\n    94→    .eq('is_active', true)\n    95→    .order('sort_order')\n    96→\n    97→  if (!categories || categories.length === 0) return []\n    98→\n    99→  // Fetch product counts by category_id\n   100→  const { data: productCounts } = await supabaseAdmin\n   101→    .from('products')\n   102→    .select('category_id')\n   103→    .eq('status', 'active')\n   104→    .not('category_id', 'is', null)\n   105→\n   106→  // Build count map\n   107→  const countMap = new Map<string, number>()\n   108→  for (const p of productCounts || []) {\n   109→    if (p.category_id) {\n   110→      countMap.set(p.category_id, (countMap.get(p.category_id) || 0) + 1)\n   111→    }\n   112→  }\n   113→\n   114→  // Build parent->children map\n   115→  const parents = categories.filter(c => !c.parent_id)\n   116→  const childrenByParent = new Map<string, typeof categories>()\n   117→  for (const c of categories) {\n   118→    if (c.parent_id) {\n   119→      const list = childrenByParent.get(c.parent_id) || []\n   120→      list.push(c)\n   121→      childrenByParent.set(c.parent_id, list)\n   122→    }\n   123→  }\n   124→\n   125→  // For each parent, calculate total count and fetch 3 preview images\n   126→  const tree = await Promise.all(parents.map(async (parent) => {\n   127→    const children = childrenByParent.get(parent.id) || []\n   128→    const allCategoryIds = [parent.id, ...children.map(c => c.id)]\n   129→    const ownCount = countMap.get(parent.id) || 0\n   130→    const childCount = children.reduce((s, c) => s + (countMap.get(c.id) || 0), 0)\n   131→    const totalCount = ownCount + childCount\n   132→\n   133→    // Fetch 3 preview product images\n   134→    const { data: previewProducts } = await supabaseAdmin\n   135→      .from('products')\n   136→      .select('images')\n   137→      .in('category_id', allCategoryIds)\n   138→      .eq('status', 'active')\n   139→      .order('avg_rating', { ascending: false })\n   140→      .limit(3)\n   141→\n   142→    const previewImages = (previewProducts || [])\n   143→      .map((p: any) => p.images?.[0]?.src)\n   144→      .filter(Boolean) as string[]\n   145→\n   146→    return {\n   147→      slug: parent.slug,\n   148→      name_en: parent.name_en,\n   149→      name_es: parent.name_es,\n   150→      name_de: parent.name_de,\n   151→      imageUrl: parent.image_url,\n   152→      totalProductCount: totalCount,\n   153→      previewImages,\n   154→    }\n   155→  }))\n   156→\n   157→  // Cache the raw tree (locale-independent)\n   158→  await setCachedCategoryTree(tree)\n   159→\n   160→  // Return localized\n   161→  return tree.map(cat => ({\n   162→    ...cat,\n   163→    name: cat[nameField] || cat.name_en,\n   164→  }))\n   165→}\n   166→\n   167→interface ShopPageProps {\n   168→  params: Promise<{ locale: string }>\n   169→  searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>\n   170→}\n   171→\n   172→type SortOption = 'featured' | 'priceLowToHigh' | 'priceHighToLow' | 'newest' | 'topRated'\n   173→\n   174→const PRODUCTS_PER_PAGE = 20\n   175→\n   176→// ISR: revalidate shop page every 5 minutes (matches category tree Redis TTL)\n   177→export const revalidate = 300\n   178→\n   179→// Server Component - generates metadata for SEO\n   180→export async function generateMetadata({ params, searchParams }: ShopPageProps): Promise<Metadata> {\n   181→  const { locale } = await params\n   182→  const search = await searchParams\n   183→  const t = await getTranslations({ locale, namespace: 'shop' })\n   184→\n   185→  const baseUrl = BASE_URL\n   186→  const siteName = BRAND.name\n   187→\n   188→  const query = search.q as string | undefined\n   189→\n   190→  let title = `${t('title')} - ${siteName}`\n   191→  let description = t('subtitle')\n   192→\n   193→  if (query) {\n   194→    title = `Search: ${query} - ${siteName}`\n   195→    description = `Search results for \"${query}\" in our custom print-on-demand products`\n   196→  }\n   197→\n   198→  return {\n   199→    title,\n   200→   21→]\n    22→\n    23→// Create the i18n middleware\n    24→const intlMiddleware = createMiddleware(routing)\n    25→\n    26→// Simple string hash for deterministic A/B variant assignment\n    27→function simpleHash(str: string): number {\n    28→  let hash = 0\n    29→  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {\n    30→    const char = str.charCodeAt(i)\n    31→    hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + char\n    32→    hash |= 0 // Convert to 32-bit integer\n    33→  }\n    34→  return hash\n    35→}\n    36→\n    37→// PRIMARY_DOMAINS imported from @/lib/store-config (canonical source)\n    38→\n function middleware(request: NextRequest) {\n    43→  // Get the pathname early for routing decisions\n    44→  const pathname = request.nextUrl.pathname\n    45→  const hostname = request.nextUrl.hostname\n    46→\n    47→  // --- Tenant Resolution (custom domain → tenant_id) ---\n    48→  // Edge-compatible: calls /api/tenant-resolve (server route with Redis cache)\n    49→  // so we avoid ioredis (Node-only) in the Edge runtime.\n    50→  const requestHeaders = new Headers(request.headers)\n    51→\n    52→  const isPrimaryDomain = PRIMARY_DOMAINS.some(\n    53→    (d) => hostname === d || hostname.endsWith(`.${d}`)\n    54→  )\n    55→  const skipTenantResolution = SKIP_TENANT_PATHS.some((p) => pathname.startsWith(p))\n    56→\n    57→  // Check cookie cache first to avoid HTTP roundtrip on every request\n    58→  const cachedTenantId = request.cookies.get('x-tenant-id')?.value\n    59→  if (cachedTenantId) {\n    60→    requestHeaders.set('x-tenant-id', cachedTenantId)\n    61→  } else if (!isPrimaryDomain && !skipTenantResolution) {\n    62→    try {\n    63→      const resolveUrl = new URL(\n    64→        `/api/tenant-resolve?domain=${encodeURIComponent(hostname)}`,\n    65→        request.url\n    66→      )\n    67→      const tenantRes = await fetch(resolveUrl.toString())\n    68→      if (tenantRes.ok) {\n    69→        const data = await tenantRes.json()\n    70→        if (data.tenant_id) {\n    71→          requestHeaders.set('x-tenant-id', data.tenant_id)\n    72→        }\n    73→      }\n    74→    } catch {\n    75→      // Tenant resolution failed — continue without tenant isolation\n    76→    }\n    77→  }\n    78→  // --- End Tenant Resolution ---\n    79→\n    80→  // Skip i18n middleware for API routes (they don't have locale prefixes)\n    81→  const isApiRoute = pathname.startsWith('/api')\n    82→\n    83→  // Build response — API routes get modified request headers forwarded downstream;\n    84→  // page routes go through i18n middleware and receive tenant header via response headers.\n    85→  let response: NextResponse\n    86→  if (isApiRoute) {\n    87→    // Forward modified request headers to API route handlers\n    88→    response = NextResponse.next({ request: { headers: requestHeaders } })\n    89→  } else {\n    90→    response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse\n    91→    // Propagate tenant ID for Server Components (Next.js forwards response headers)\n    92→    const tenantId = requestHeaders.get('x-tenant-id')\n ---\n   109→  // Get or create visitor ID for deterministic variant assignment\n   110→  let visitorId = request.cookies.get('pod-visitor-id')?.value\n   111→  if (!visitorId) {\n   112→    visitorId = crypto.randomUUID()\n   113→    response.cookies.set('pod-visitor-id', visitorId, {\n   114→      httpOnly: true,\n   115→      secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',\n   116→      sameSite: 123→  const abConfigRaw = request.cookies.get('__ab_config')?.value\n   124→  const activeVariants: Record<string, string> = {}\n   125→\n   126→  if (abConfigRaw) {\n   127→    try {\n   128→ {"parentUuid":"b4559a97-433c-4db7-ba07-285a91297bf2","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a70b9d5fbb31d3fbf","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TxQaX3NASwGbQEN1uk6VTW","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→import { getRequestConfig } from 'next-intl/server'\n     2→import { routing } from './routing'\n     3→\n     4→export default getRequestConfig(async ({ requestLocale }) => {\n     5→  let locale = await requestLocale\n     6→\n     7→  // Ensure that the incoming locale is valid\n     8→  if (!locale || !routing.locales.includes(locale as any)) {\n     9→    locale = routing.defaultLocale\n    10→  }\n    11→\n    12→  // Load messages from file\n    13→  // Note: Redis caching for translations is not feasible in Edge runtime\n    14→  // where getRequestConfig runs. Translation files are small and Next.js\n    15→  // already caches dynamic imports effectively.\n    16→  let messages = (await import(`../../messages/${locale}.json`)).default\n    17→\n    18→  // For non-English locales, merge with English as fallback\n    19→  if (locale !== 'en') {\n    20→    const englishMessages = (await import(`../../messages/en.json`)).default\n    21→    messages = { ...englishMessages, ...messages }\n    22→  }\n    23→\n    24→  return {\n    25→    locale,\n    26→    messages,\n    27→  }\n    28→})\n    29→"}]},"uuid":"db21fc2f-84d7-4fa9-96d4-eb24498009db","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:38:25.486Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b4559a97-433c-4db7-ba07-285a91297bf2"}

6→\n     7→function hasWebGL(): boolean {\n     8→  try {\n     9→    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')\n    10→    return !!(canvas.getContext('webgl') || canvas.getContext('webgl2'))\n    11→  } catch {\n    12→    return false\n    13→  }\n    14→}\n    15→\n    16→/** Resolve any CSS color (including oklch) to hex via canvas */\n    17→function cssColorToHex(cssColor: string): string {\n    18→  if (cssColor.startsWith('#')) return cssColor\n    19→  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')\n    20→  canvas.width = canvas.height = 1\n    21→  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')\n    22→  if (!ctx) return '#000000'\n    23→  ctx.fillStyle = cssColor\n    24→  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 1, 1)\n    25→  const [r, g, b] = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, 1, 1).data\n    26→  return `#${((1 << 24) + (r << 16) + (g << 8) + b).toString(16).slice(1)}`\n    27→}\n    28→\n    29→/** Read a CSS variable from :root and resolve to hex */\n    30→function getThemeColorHex(varName: string, fallback: string): string {\n    31→  const raw = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(varName).trim()\n    32→  if (!raw) return fallback\n    33→  return cssColorToHex(raw)\n    34→}\n    35→\n    36→interface Colors {\n    37→  back: string\n    38→  blob1: string\n    39→  blob2: string\n    40→  blob3: string\n    41→}\n    42→\n    43→function readColorsFromDOM(isDark: boolean): Colors {\n    44→  return {\n    45→    back: getThemeColorHex('--background', isDark ? => {\n    15→    getCachedProducts().then(setProducts).catch(() => {})\n    16→  }, [])\n    17→\n    18→  return (\n    19→    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh] px-4\">\n    20→      <WifiOff className=\"h-16 w-16 text-muted-foreground mb-4\" />\n    21→      <h1 className=\"text-2xl font-bold mb-2\">{t('title')}</h1>\n    22→      <p className=\"text-muted-foreground text-center mb-6 max-w-md\">\n    23→        {t('description')}\n    24→      </p>\n    25→      <Button onClick={() => window.location.reload()} variant=\"outline\" className=\"gap-2\">\n    26→        <RefreshCw className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n    27→        {t('tryAgain')}\n    28→      </Button>\n    29→\n    30→      {products.length > 0 && (\n    31→        <div className=\"mt-8 w-full max-w-4xl\">\n    32→          <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold mb-4\">{t('cachedProducts')}</h2>\n    33→          <div className=\"grid grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(200px,1fr))] gap-4\">\n    34→            {products.slice(0, 12).map((product) => (\n    35→              <Card key={String(product.id)} 'Product')}</p>\n    38→                  <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n    39→                    &euro;{Number(product.price || 0).toFixed(2)}\n    40→                  </p>\n {"parentUuid":"594f0a71-fd24-4e06-9718-c05888e7fa40","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a70b9d5fbb31d3fbf","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RWcyHc1E2ZTWGRAKY8w88k","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Locale-aware currency formatting utilities\n     3→ */\n     4→\n     5→import { LOCALE_CURRENCY, LOCALE_FORMAT, STORE_DEFAULTS } from './store-config'\n     6→\n     7→/**\n     8→ * Get the default currency for a given locale\n     9→ */\n    10→export function getCurrencyForLocale(locale: string): string {\n    11→  return LOCALE_CURRENCY[locale] || STORE_DEFAULTS.currency\n    12→}\n    13→\n    14→/**\n    15→ * Get the Intl.NumberFormat locale code for a given locale\n    16→ */\n    17→export function getFormatLocale(locale: string): string {\n    18→  return LOCALE_FORMAT[locale] || LOCALE_FORMAT['en']\n    19→}\n    20→\n    21→/**\n    22→ * Format a price amount in the appropriate currency for the given locale\n    23→ *\n    24→ * @param price - The numeric price to format\n    25→ * @param locale - The current locale (en, es, de)\n    26→ * @param currency - Optional currency override (defaults to locale's currency)\n    27→ * @returns Formatted price string (e.g., \"24,99 €\")\n    28→ */\n    29→export function formatPrice(\n    30→  price: number,\n    31→  locale: string,\n    32→  currency?: string\n    33→): string {\n    34→  const currencyCode = currency || getCurrencyForLocale(locale)\n    35→  const formatLocale = getFormatLocale(locale)\n    36→\n    37→  return new Intl.NumberFormat(formatLocale, {\n    38→    style: 'currency',\n    39→    currency: currencyCode,\n    40→  }).format(price)\n    41→}\n    42→\n    43→/**\n    44→ * Get the currency symbol for a given currency code\n    45→ *\n    46→ * @param currency - ISO 4217 currency code (e.g., 'EUR', 'USD')\n    47→ * @param locale - Locale for formatting (defaults to 'en')\n    48→ * @returns Currency symbol (e.g., '€', '$')\n    49→ */\n    50→export function getCurrencySymbol(currency: string, locale: string = 'en'): string {\n    51→  const formatLocale = getFormatLocale(locale)\n    52→  return new Intl.NumberFormat(formatLocale, {\n    53→    style: 'currency',\n    54→    currency,\n    55→    minimumFractionDigits: 0,\n    56→    maximumFractionDigits: 0,\n    57→  }).format(0).replace(/[\\d\\s.,]/g, '').trim()\n    58→}\n    59→\n    60→/**\n    61→ * Convert a price from one currency to another (placeholder for future API integration)\n    62→ * For now, uses simplified conversion rates rebased to EUR\n    63→ *\n    64→ * @param price - The price to convert\n    65→ * @param fromCurrency - Source currency code\n    66→ * @param toCurrency - Target currency code\n    67→ * @returns Converted price\n    68→ */\n    69→export function convertPrice(\n    70→  price: number,\n    71→  fromCurrency: string,\n    72→  toCurrency: string\n    73→): number {\n    74→  // Simplified conversion rates relative to EUR (in production, use real-time exchange rates API)\n    75→  const rates: Record<string, number> = {\n    76→    EUR: 1.0,\n    77→    USD: 1.09,\n    78→    GBP: 0.86,\n    79→  }\n    80→\n    81→  if (fromCurrency === toCurrency) {\n    82→    return price\n    83→  }\n    84→\n    85→  // Convert to EUR first, then to target currency\n    86→  const eurAmount = price / (rates[fromCurrency] || 1)\n    87→  return eurAmount * (rates[toCurrency] || 1)\n    88→}\n    89→\n    90→/**\n    91→ * Get the price for a product in the user's locale currency\n    92→ *\n    93→ * @param basePrice - The base price (typically in EUR)\n    94→ * @param baseCurrency - The currency of the base price\n    95→ * @param locale - The current locale\n    96→ * @returns Price converted to locale's currency\n    97→ */\n    98→export function getLocalizedPrice(\n    99→  basePrice: number,\n   100→  baseCurrency: string,\n   101→  locale: string\n   102→): number {\n   103→  const targetCurrency = getCurrencyForLocale(locale)\n   104→  return convertPrice(basePrice, baseCurrency, targetCurrency)\n   105→}\n   106→"}]},"uuid":"35766e5f-d841-4271-b245-16977688ded4","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:39:51.701Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"594f0a71-fd24-4e06-9718-c05888e7fa40"}
